\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jli}\right\|\leq\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\varphi_{jli}^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}

with

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\varphi_{jli}^{2}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right)^{2}\leq\dfrac{1}{T}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{jl}\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right)^{2}\leq O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\right),

by Assumption
2
and Assumption
6
(c), so that taking into account Lemma
1
we have

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jli}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}}\right)O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

It follows that

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jli}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

which completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Proof of Lemma
3
.

Consider

\begin{array}[]{rcl}N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}&=&N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}+N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\\
&=&N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}+N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right).\end{array}

(B.8)

Using the identity in (
B.2
), we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{A}&=&\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{-1}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{i}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{i}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}\\
&=&\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{-1}\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\sigma_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right]+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\chi_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right]\\
+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right]+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jli}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right]\end{array}\right\}.\end{array}

(B.9)

Consider

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\sigma_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\sigma_{jil}\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\sigma_{jil}.

We have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\sigma_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\sigma_{jil}\right|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}}\right)O_{p}\left(1\right)O_{p}\left(1\right)\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right),\end{array}

by Lemma
1
, Assumption
2
, and the fact that, given
\rho_{jil}=\sigma_{jil}\left/\left(\sigma_{jii}\sigma_{jll}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\right.
, by Assumption
3
(b) we have

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\sigma_{jil}\right|^{2}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sigma_{jii}\sigma_{jll}\rho_{jil}^{2}\leq M\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\sigma_{jii}\sigma_{jll}\right|^{1\left/2\right.}\left|\rho_{jil}\right|=M\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\sigma_{jil}\right|\leq M^{2}.

(B.10)

Further

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\sigma_{jil}\right\|\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|\left|\sigma_{jil}\right|\right)=O\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\right)

by Assumptions
2
and
3
(b). Therefore,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\sigma_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right)+O\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right).

(B.11)

Consider now

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\chi_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\chi_{jil}\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\chi_{jil}.

We have

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\chi_{jil}\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\chi_{jil}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|

and consider

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\chi_{jil}\right\|\leq\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\chi_{jil}\right|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}

with

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\chi_{jil}\right|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}&=&\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right|^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
3
(c). Therefore, taking into account Lemma
1
,

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\chi_{jil}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}}\right)O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

Further,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\chi_{jil}\right\|&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right]\right\|\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{a}_{i}\left[\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right]\right\|\right\}\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left\{\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{a}_{i}\left[\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right]\right\|^{2}\right\}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
and
6
(a). Therefore,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\chi_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

(B.12)

Consider now

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jil}\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\varphi_{jil}\right).

We have

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jil}\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|\leq\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\varphi_{jil}\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}

and

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\varphi_{jil}\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}&=&\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{lt}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{lt}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{lt}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
and
6
(b). Therefore,

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jil}\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}}\right)O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}C_{NT}}\right)

by Lemma
1
. Further

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\varphi_{jil}\right)\right\|&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{lt}\right)\right\|\\
&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{1l}\\
\bm{\lambda}_{2l}\end{array}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{lt}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}\\
\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\end{array}\right)^{\prime}\right\|\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{1l}\\
\bm{\lambda}_{2l}\end{array}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{f}_{jt}^{\prime}e_{lt}\right)\right\|\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{ji}\right\|\left\|\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}\\
\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\end{array}\right)^{\prime}\right\|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
and
6
(b). Therefore

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jil}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right).

(B.13)

Finally,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jli}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jli}\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\varphi_{jli}\right).

We have

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jli}\right]\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\right\|\leq\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\varphi_{jli}\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}

with

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\varphi_{jli}\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}&=&\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{i}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
and
6
(b). Further

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\varphi_{jli}\right)\right\|&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right)\right\|\\
&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{1l}\\
\bm{\lambda}_{2l}\end{array}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\\
\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right\|\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{1l}\\
\bm{\lambda}_{2l}\end{array}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{f}_{jt}^{\prime}e_{it}\right)\right\|\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{jl}\right\|\left\|\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\\
\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right\|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
and
6
(b). Therefore,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jli}\right)\mathbf{a}_{i}^{\prime}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right).

(B.14)

Combining equations (
B.9
) through (
B.14
), we obtain

N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{A}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).

(B.15)

From (
B.8
), (
B.15
) and Lemma
1
, we obtain

N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).

which completes the proof of the lemma.

∎

Proof of Lemma
4
.

Given the identity in (
B.2
), we can write

\begin{array}[]{rcl}N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}&=&\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{-1}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{i}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{i}\right)e_{it}\\
&=&\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{-1}\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\sigma_{jil}\right)e_{it}\right]+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\chi_{jil}\right)e_{it}\right]\\
+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jil}\right)e_{it}\right]+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jli}\right)e_{it}\right]\end{array}\right\}.\end{array}

(B.16)

Consider

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}\sigma_{jil}\right)e_{it}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\sigma_{jil}\right]e_{it}+\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\sigma_{jil}e_{it}\right),

where

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\sigma_{jil}\right]e_{it}\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\sigma_{jil}\right|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&&\times\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|e_{it}\right|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}}\right)O_{p}\left(1\right)O_{p}\left(1\right)\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right)\end{array}

by Lemma
1
, equation (
B.10
), and Assumption
3
(a), and

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\sigma_{jil}e_{it}\right)\right\|&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathsf{E}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)e_{it}\right\|\\
&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)e_{it}\right\|\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{N}\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right|\right]\left\|\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|\left|e_{it}\right|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
(a), Assumption
3
(a), and Assumption
3
(b), so that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}\sigma_{jil}\right)e_{it}\right\|&\leq&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\sigma_{jil}\right]e_{it}\right\|\\
&&+\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\sigma_{jil}e_{it}\right)\right\|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right).\end{array}

(B.17)

Consider now

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\chi_{jil}\right)e_{it}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\chi_{jil}\right]e_{it}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}e_{it}\chi_{jil}.

We have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\chi_{jil}\right]e_{it}\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\right\|\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\chi_{jil}e_{it}\right|\right)\\
&\leq&\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\chi_{jil}e_{it}\right|\right)^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\end{array}

with

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\chi_{jil}e_{it}\right|&=&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right]e_{it}\right|\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\left[\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right]e_{it}\right|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
3
(a) and
3
(c). Therefore, taking into account Lemma
1
,

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\chi_{jil}\right]e_{it}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}}\right)O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

Further,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\chi_{jil}e_{it}\right\|&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right]e_{it}\right\|\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{a}_{l}\left[\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right]\right\|\left|e_{it}\right|\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\{\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{a}_{l}\left[\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right]\right\|^{2}\right\}^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|e_{it}\right|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
3
(a) and
6
(a). Therefore,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\chi_{jil}\right)e_{it}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

(B.18)

Consider now

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jil}\right)e_{it}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jil}\right]e_{it}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\varphi_{jil}e_{it}.

We have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jil}\right]e_{it}\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\varphi_{jil}e_{it}\right|\right)\\
&\leq&\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\varphi_{jil}e_{it}\right|\right)^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.},\end{array}

with

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\varphi_{jil}e_{it}\right|&=&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{lt}\right)e_{it}\right|\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{ji}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right\|\left|e_{lt}\right|\\
&\leq&\bar{\lambda}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left|e_{lt}\right|\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
,
3
(a) and
4
. Taking into account Lemma
1
,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jil}\right]e_{it}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}}\right)O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

Further,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\varphi_{jil}e_{it}\right\|&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right)e_{lt}\right\|\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|\left|e_{lt}\right|\right)\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right\|\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|e_{lt}\right|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right\|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
,
3
(a) and
6
(a). Therefore,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{\widehat{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jil}\right)e_{it}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

(B.19)

Finally,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jli}\right)e_{it}=\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jli}\right]e_{it}+\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\varphi_{jli}e_{it}.

Consider first

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jli}\right]e_{it}\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left[\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\varphi_{jli}e_{it}\right|\right)\right]\\
&\leq&\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\varphi_{jli}e_{it}\right|\right)^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.},\end{array}

with

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\varphi_{jli}e_{it}\right|&=&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right)e_{it}\right|\\
&\leq&\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{jl}\right\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right\|\left|e_{it}\right|\right)\\
&\leq&\bar{\lambda}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{f}_{jt}e_{it}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left|e_{it}\right|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\end{array}

by Assumptions
2
(a),
3
(a), and
4
, so that

\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{a}_{l}\right)\varphi_{jli}\right]e_{it}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

Also,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\varphi_{jli}e_{it}\right\|&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{v=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jv}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jv}e_{iv}\right)e_{it}\right\|\\
&=&\left\|\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{v=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jv}\mathbf{f}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)\right\|\\
&\leq&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\mathbf{a}_{l}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{v=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{jv}\mathbf{f}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right\|\\
&=&\left\|\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{v=1}^{T}\mathbf{f}_{jv}\left[\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}-\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)+\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)\right]\right\|O\left(1\right)\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{v=1}^{T}\left\{\left\|\mathbf{f}_{jv}\right\|\left[\left|\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}-\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)\right|+\left|\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)\right|\right]\right\}O\left(1\right)\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{v=1}^{T}\left[\left|\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}-\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)\right|+\left|\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)\right|\right]O_{p}\left(1\right)\\
&\leq&\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{v=1}^{T}\left|\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}-\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)\right|\right]O_{p}\left(1\right)\\
&&+\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{v=1}^{T}\left|\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jv}e_{iv}e_{it}\right)\right|\right]O_{p}\left(1\right)\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\right)\end{array}

by Assumption
3
(c). Therefore,

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\mathbf{a}}_{l}\varphi_{jli}\right)e_{it}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right).

(B.20)

By combining (
B.16
) through (
B.20
), we have

N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C^{2}_{NT}}\right),

which completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Proof of Lemma
5
.

Starting from (a), and taking into account (
13
), consider

\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}=N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{t}=N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{Ag}_{t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)=N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{Ag}_{t}+N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}

and note that

\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1}+\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1},

so that we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}&=&N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1}+\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{g}_{t}+N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}\\
&=&N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1}+\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{g}_{t}+N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}+N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}-N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}\\
&=&N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{g}_{t}+N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}+N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}+N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t},\end{array}

which leads to

\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}=N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}+N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{H}}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{g}_{t}+N^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}.

(B.21)

The result in (a) follows by taking into account Assumption
6
(d), Lemma
3
and Lemma
4
. As for (b), adding and subtracting terms we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}&=&\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}.\end{array}

(B.22)

Taking into account the results in (a), it follows that

\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)^{\prime}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{4}}\right).

(B.23)

From (
B.21
), we also have that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}&=&\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\left(\mathbf{A}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}+\dfrac{1}{N}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{A-}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{g}_{t}+\dfrac{1}{N}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right]\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{A-}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right)}{N}\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left(\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{\sqrt{N}}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right),\end{array}

and taking into account Assumptions
2
and
6
(c), and Lemma
3
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\|\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{A}}{\sqrt{N}}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\\
&&+\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{A-}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right)}{N}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\|\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}}{\sqrt{N}}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).\end{array}

(B.24)

Combining (
B.22
) through (
B.24
), it follows that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{4}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right),\end{array}

which shows (b) and completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Proof of Lemma
6
.

We proceed by following steps analogous to those in the proof of Proposition 1 in
Bai 2003
, and we develop the proof of the lemma for the sake of completeness. Given
\mathbf{\widehat{H}}=\left(\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}/T\right)\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}/N\right)\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{-1}
, pre-multiply both sides of the identity
\left(1/NT\right)\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\mathbf{X\widehat{A}}=\mathbf{\widehat{A}\widehat{V}}

by
\left(\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}\left/T\right.\right)^{1\left/2\right.}N^{-1}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}
to obtain

\frac{1}{N}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\mathbf{X}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}}{NT}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{A}}=\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{V}.}

Given
\left(\ref{eq:linear_model}\right)
, write

\mathbf{X}=\mathbf{G}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}

with
\mathbf{X}=\left(\mathbf{x}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{x}_{T}\right)^{\prime}
and
\mathbf{E}=\left(\mathbf{e}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{e}_{T}\right)^{\prime}
. We thus have

\dfrac{1}{N}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{AGG}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}}{NT}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{A}}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}}=\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{V}},

(B.25)

where

\mathbf{\widehat{D}}=\dfrac{1}{N}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{AGE+E}^{\prime}\mathbf{G}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}}{NT}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{A}}=o_{p}\left(1\right)

by Lemma
2
.
Let

\begin{array}[]{ll}\mathbf{W}=\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}}{N}\right)\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.},&\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}=\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\right),\end{array}

so that we can write (
B.25
) as

\left(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}=\mathbf{\widehat{Z}\widehat{V}}.

Therefore, each column of
\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}
is an eigenvector of
\left(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1}\right)
, with length different from unity. Let
\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{\ast}
be the diagonal matrix of the diagonal elements of
\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}
. Define
\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}=\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{\ast}\right)^{-1\left/2\right.}
so that each column of
\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}
has unit length. We thus get

\left(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}=\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}\widehat{V}},

where
\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}
is the eigenvector matrix of
\left(\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1}\right)
. Consider

\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1}=\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}}{N}\right)\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1},

and note that

\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}=\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\\
\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\\
\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\end{array}\right)^{\prime}=\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}\end{array}\right)=\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}

by Assumption 1. Further,
\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}\left/N\right.\right)\rightarrow\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}
by Assumption 2.
Therefore, by Assumptions 1 and 2,
\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{1\left/2\right.}
. Because the eigenvalues of
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{1\left/2\right.}
are distinct by
Assumption
5
, the eigenvalues of
\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1}
are also distinct for large
N
and
T
, by the continuity of eigenvalues. This implies that the eigenvector matrix of
\mathbf{W}+\mathbf{\widehat{D}\widehat{Z}}^{-1}
is unique except for the fact that each column can be replaced by its negative value. Further, the
p-th
column of
\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}
depends on
\mathbf{\widehat{A}}
only through the
p-th
column of
\mathbf{\widehat{A}}
, for
p=1,\ldots,r
. This implies that the sign of each column in

\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}
, and thus in
\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}=\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{\ast}\right)^{-1\left/2\right.}
, is determined by the
sign of the corresponding column of
\mathbf{\widehat{A}}
. Therefore, the
column sign of
\mathbf{\widehat{A}}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}
are uniquely
determined. By the eigenvector perturbation theory, which requires the
eigenvalues to be distinct, there exists a unique eigenvector matrix
\mathbf{\Psi}
of
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{A}}^{1\left/2\right.}
such that
\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}-\mathbf{\Psi}\right\|=o_{p}\left(1\right)
. Since
\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}=\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{\ast}\right)^{-1\left/2\right.}

and
\mathbf{\widehat{Z}}=\left(\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}\left/T\right.\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}\left/N\right.\right)
then
\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}=\left(\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}\left/T\right.\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}\left/N\right.\right)\left(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{\ast}\right)^{-1\left/2\right.}
, which implies that

\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}=\left(\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\right)^{-1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{\Psi}}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{\ast}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{-1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi V}^{1\left/2\right.}

by Assumption 1 and since
\mathbf{\widehat{V}}^{\ast}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mathbf{V}
, the latter following from arguments analogous to those in the proof of Proposition 1 in
Bai 2003
. This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Proof of Lemma
7
.

From Lemma
6
, and taking into account (
28
), we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{Q}&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{Q}_{1}\\
\mathbf{Q}_{2}\end{array}\right)\\
&=&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{-1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi V}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}1}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}2}\end{array}\right)^{-1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi V}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\Psi}_{1}\\
\mathbf{\Psi}_{2}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{V}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}1}^{-1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{1}\mathbf{V}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{2}\mathbf{V}^{1\left/2\right.}\end{array}\right)\end{array},

which completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Proof of Lemma
8
.

Given the equivalent linear representation in (
13
), we can write

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}&=&\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{Ag}_{t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)\left(\mathbf{Ag}_{t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)^{\prime}\\
&=&\dfrac{\mathbf{A}}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{A}}{\sqrt{N}}^{\prime}+\dfrac{\mathbf{A}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\\
&&+\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}}{N}+\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}.\end{array}

(B.26)

Taking into account Assumption
2
(b) and Assumption
4
, it follows that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{A}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{A}}{\sqrt{N}}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\\
\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right\|\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}O_{p}\left(1\right)O_{p}\left(\sqrt{N}\right)\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).\end{array}

(B.27)

Similarly, we can prove that

\dfrac{1}{N}\mathbf{A}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(B.28)

Finally, by the weak dependence condition in Assumption (
3
),

\left\|\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right\|=o_{p}\left(1\right).

(B.29)

By combining (
B.26
) through (
B.29
), we then have

\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}=\dfrac{\mathbf{A}}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{N}}+o_{p}\left(1\right)=\dfrac{\mathbf{A}}{\sqrt{N}}\dfrac{\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}}{T}\dfrac{\mathbf{A}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{N}}+o_{p}\left(1\right).

The result in the lemma follows from Assumptions (
1
) and (
2
) by noting that the eigenvalues of

\left(\mathbf{A}\left/\sqrt{N}\right.\right)\left(\mathbf{GG}^{\prime}\left/T\right.\right)\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\left/\sqrt{N}\right.\right)
are the same as those of

\left(\mathbf{G}^{\prime}\left/\sqrt{T}\right.\right)\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}\left/N\right.\right)\left(\mathbf{G}\left/\sqrt{T}\right.\right)
.
∎

Proof of Lemma
9
.

From the definition of
\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}k_{1}}
in (
39
), and taking into account Lemma (
5
)(a), we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}k_{1}}&=&\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\\
&=&\left\{\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right]\\
\times\left[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right]^{\prime}\end{array}\right\}\right\}\\
&&\times\left\{\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right]\\
\times\left[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right]^{\prime}\end{array}\right\}\right\}^{-1}\\
&=&\left\{\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\left[\begin{array}[]{l}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\\
+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{4}}\right)\end{array}\right]\right\}\\
&&\times\left\{\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\left[\begin{array}[]{l}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\\
+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}^{2}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{4}}\right)\end{array}\right]\right\}^{-1}\\
&=&\left[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right]\\
&&\times\left[\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right]^{-1}\\
&=&\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}+o_{p}\left(1\right).\end{array}

Taking further into account the definition of
\mathbf{g}_{t}
in (
11
), it follows that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}k_{1}}&=&\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{g}_{t}\mathbf{g}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}+o_{p}\left(1\right)\\
&=&\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\\
\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\\
\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\end{array}\right)^{\prime}\right]\\
&&\times\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\\
\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\\
\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\end{array}\right)^{\prime}\right]^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}+o_{p}\left(1\right)\\
&=&\widehat{\mathbf{H}}^{-1}\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right]\\
&&\times\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}_{j,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right]^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}+o_{p}\left(1\right)\\
&=&\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbb{I}\left(j=1\right)\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbb{I}\left(j=2\right)\mathbf{I}_{r_{2}}\end{array}\right]\mathbf{H}+o_{p}\left(1\right),\end{array}

where the last equality follows from (
38
). Therefore,

\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}k_{1}}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mathbf{H}^{-1}\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbb{I}\left(j=1\right)\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbb{I}\left(j=2\right)\mathbf{I}_{r_{2}}\end{array}\right]\mathbf{H},

which completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Proof of Lemma
10
.

From the definitions of eigenvectors and eigenvalues, for
j=1,2
it follows that

\widehat{\mathbf{\Sigma}}_{\widehat{\xi},\mathbf{x}j}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}=\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)},

and, given the definition of
\widehat{\mathbf{\Sigma}}_{\widehat{\xi},\mathbf{x}j}
in (
47
), we can write

\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}=\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}.

(B.30)

The normalisation constraint

\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}}{N}=\mathbf{I}_{p}

(B.31)

allows us to obtain

\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}=\widehat{\mathbf{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}.

Taking into account Assumption
2
(b), we then have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|&\leq&\left\|\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}}{\sqrt{N}}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}}{\sqrt{N}}\right\|\\
&=&\left\|\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|O_{p}\left(1\right).\end{array}

(B.32)

Consider now

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}&=&\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\\
&=&\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\\
&=&\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\\
&&+\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\\
&&+\dfrac{\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}.\end{array}

(B.33)

By Assumptions
1
(b) and
2
(b), it follows that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|&=&\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\dfrac{\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)}{T}\right\|\\
&\leq&\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}}{T}\right\|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(1\right).\end{array}

(B.34)

In a similar way, it can be proved that

\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|=O_{p}\left(1\right).

(B.35)

Assumptions
2
(b) implies that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{\sqrt{N}}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right\|\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right\|O_{p}\left(1\right),\end{array}

(B.36)

and, taking into account Assumption
4
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right\|&=&\left\{\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)^{\prime}\right]\right\}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left\{\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)^{\prime}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\right]\right\}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left\{\mathrm{tr}\left[\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\right]\right\}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left\{\mathrm{tr}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}e_{1t}}{\sqrt{NT}}\\
\vdots\\
\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}e_{Nt}}{\sqrt{NT}}\end{array}\right)\\
\times\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{1t}}{\sqrt{NT}}&\cdots&\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{Nt}}{\sqrt{NT}}\end{array}\right)\end{array}\right]\right\}^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left[\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}e_{it}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{it}e_{it}}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}e_{it}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{it}e_{it}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left[\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{it}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(1\right),\end{array}

(B.37)

and taking into account (
B.36
) and (
B.37
),

\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(B.38)

In a similar way, it can be proved that

\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

(B.39)

\left\|\dfrac{\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

(B.40)

and

\left\|\dfrac{\left(\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(B.41)

Finally, by Assumption
3
(b),

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\left\|\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|\left\|\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\\
&\leq&\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\left(N^{-1\left/2\right.}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|+N^{-1\left/2\right.}\left\|\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|\right)^{2}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\\
&=&O_{p}\left(1\right).\end{array}

(B.42)

By combining equations (
B.33
), (
B.34
), (
B.35
), (
B.38
), (
B.39
), (
B.40
), (
B.41
) and (
B.42
), it follows that

\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}=O_{p}\left(1\right),

which, taking into account (
B.32
), implies that

\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\prime}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}=O_{p}\left(1\right).

The result stated in the lemma then follows directly from (
B.30
) and (
B.31
).
∎

Proof of Lemma
11
.

Let
\rho_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}=\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\left/\left(\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkii}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkll}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\right.
such that
\left|\rho_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right|\leq 1
. Since
\left|\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkii}\right|\leq M<\infty
by Assumption
3
(c), then

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}^{2}&=&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\rho_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}^{2}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkii}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkll}\\
&\leq&MN^{-1}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkii}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkll}\right|^{1\left/2\right.}\left|\rho_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right|\\
&=&MN^{-1}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkii}\right|\\
&\leq&MT^{-1}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[N^{-1}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left|\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{it}e_{lt}\right)\right|\right]\\
&\leq&M^{2},\end{array}

by Assumption
3
(b), which completes the proof of the lemma.

∎

B.3
Proof of Theorem
1

Given the specification in
\left(\ref{eq:model}\right)
, from Section
2.2
recall
\mathbf{B}_{1}=[\bm{\Lambda}_{1}\ \mathbf{0}]
and
\mathbf{B}_{2}=[\mathbf{0}\ \bm{\Lambda}_{2}]
. Adding and subtracting terms, we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{x}_{t}&=&\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{g}_{t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{g}_{t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}\\
&=&\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}\widehat{g}}_{t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}\widehat{g}}_{t}+\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)+\mathbf{e}_{t},\end{array}

(B.43)

where
\mathbf{\widehat{H}}
is defined in (
35
), and
\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}
is the estimator for
\mathbf{g}_{t}
given in (
23
). We focus upon
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}=\left[\mathbf{\widehat{b}}_{11},\ldots,\mathbf{\widehat{b}}_{1N}\right]^{\prime}
as an estimator for
\mathbf{B}_{1}=\left[\mathbf{b}_{11},\ldots,\mathbf{b}_{1N}\right]^{\prime}
: analogous arguments hold for
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}
. From (
24
), and taking into account (
B.43
), we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&=&\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\\
&=&\left\{\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\left[\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}\widehat{g}}_{t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}\widehat{g}}_{t}+\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)+\mathbf{e}_{t}\right]\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right\}\\
&&\times\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\\
&=&\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}+\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\\
&&+\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left[\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right]\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\\
&&+\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left[\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right]\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\\
&&+\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}.\end{array}

Since
\mathbb{I}_{2t}=1-\mathbb{I}_{1t}
, and recalling the definition of
\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}
in (
39
), after some algebra we get

\begin{array}[]{rrl}\sqrt{T}\left[\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}\right)\right]&=&\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\\
&&+\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left[\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right]\\
&&\times\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}\\
&&+\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left[\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right]\\
&&\times\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}.\end{array}

(B.44)

For
0<M<\infty
, and taking into account Lemma
5
(b), for
j=1,2
we have that,

\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\leq M\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{-1}\mathbf{g}_{t}-\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right]=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).

(B.45)

From (
B.44
) and (
B.45
), and taking into account Assumption
7
, it follows that

\sqrt{T}\left[\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}\right)\right]=\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}^{\prime}\right)^{-1}+o_{p}\left(1\right).

Since
\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}=N^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{t}
and
\mathbf{x}_{t}=\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}
then
\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}=N^{-1}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}+\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)
. After some algebra, we have

\begin{array}[]{cl}&\sqrt{T}\left[\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}-\mathbf{B}_{1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}-\mathbf{B}_{2}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}\right)\right]\\
=&\left\{\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}+\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}+\left[\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\left(\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\right)\right]\right\}\\
&\times\left[\begin{array}[]{l}\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\\
+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\\
+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\\
+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{e}_{t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\end{array}\right]^{-1}+o_{p}\left(1\right).\end{array}

(B.46)

By Lemma
2
, and taking into account the identity in (
B.2
), it follows that

\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}^{\prime}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right),

(B.47)

which implies that

\mathbf{\widehat{A}}-\mathbf{A\widehat{H}}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}C_{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right).

(B.48)

From (
B.46
) through (
B.48
), it follows that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\sqrt{T}\left[\mathbf{\widehat{b}}_{1i}-\mathbf{\widehat{I}}^{\prime}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{b}_{1i}-\left(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\widehat{\xi}}1}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}^{\prime}\mathbf{b}_{2i}\right]\\
=&\left[\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}\right)\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}}{N}\right]^{-1}\\
&\times\left[\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{it}\right)+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{A}}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}e_{it}\right)\right]+o_{p}\left(1\right),\end{array}

and the result stated in the theorem follows by Assumption
1
and Lemma
6
, and by noting that, by Assumption
6
(c),
\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{it}\right)
and
\left(T^{-1/2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{f}_{2t}e_{it}\right)
converge in distribution to two independent Normal random variables.

B.4
Proof of Theorem
2
