Given the representation in (
12
), we can write

\mathbf{x}_{t}=\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{\xi}_{t}\otimes\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)+\mathbf{e}_{t}=\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)\left(\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\penalty\ \xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\right)^{\prime}+\mathbf{e}_{t}.

Recall also the estimators
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}
defined according to (
A.17
), with
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{j}\equiv\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{j}^{\left(k^{*}+1\right)}
, where
k^{*}
is the last iteration of the EM algorithm detailed in Section
A
. The estimators
\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}
and
\widehat{\xi}_{2,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}
for
\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}
and
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}
, respectively, are obtained as

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\\
\widehat{\xi}_{2,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\end{array}\right)&=&\left[\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)\right]^{-1}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{t}\\
&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\\
&&+\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{e}_{t}.\end{array}

Adding and subtracting terms, it follows that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\\
\widehat{\xi}_{2,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\end{array}\right)&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\\
&&+\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\\
&&-\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\\
&&+\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{e}_{t}\\
&&+\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{{}^{\prime}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{e}_{t}\\
&&-\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{{}^{\prime}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{e}_{t},\end{array}

or equivalently

\begin{array}[]{cl}&\left[\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\\
\widehat{\xi}_{2,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\end{array}\right)-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\right]\\
=&\left[N^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)\right]^{-1}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{{}^{\prime}}\left[N^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{e}_{t}\right]\\
&+\left[N^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)\right]\left\{N^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{-1}\right]\right\}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\\
&+\left[N^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}&\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\end{array}\right)\right]^{-1}\left\{N^{-1}\left[\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{{}^{\prime}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right]\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\}.\end{array}

(B.49)

Consider first

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\dfrac{1}{N}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{-1}\right]\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\\
=&\dfrac{1}{N}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)\right]\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right),\end{array}

so that from (
B.44
) and (
B.45
), and taking into account Assumption
2
, it follows that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{-1}\right]\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\right\|\\
\leq&\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left[\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)\right]\right\|\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}\right\|\left\|\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\right\|\\
=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C^{2}_{NT}}\right).\end{array}

(B.50)

By (
B.44
) and (
B.45
), and taking into account Assumption
3
(b), we also have that,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left\|\dfrac{1}{N}\left[\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{{}^{\prime}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right]\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|&\leq&\dfrac{\left\|\mathbf{e}_{t}\right\|}{\sqrt{N}}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left[\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{{}^{\prime}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\right]\right\|\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}C_{NT}^{2}}\right).\end{array}

(B.51)

Therefore, taking into account (
B.49
), (
B.50
) and (
B.51
), and by Assumption
7
, we have

\sqrt{N}\left[\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\\
\widehat{\xi}_{2,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{\widehat{g}}_{t}\end{array}\right)-\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{-1}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\xi_{1t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\\
\xi_{2t}\mathbf{g}_{t}\end{array}\right)\right]=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}}{N}&\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}}{N}\\
\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}}{N}&\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}}{N}\end{array}\right)^{-1}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{e}_{t}+o_{p}\left(1\right).

Given
\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\mathbf{\xi}}
, recall
\mathbf{I}_{\mathbf{\xi}j}=\textrm{p}\lim\nolimits_{N,T\rightarrow\infty}\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\xi}j}
for
j=1,2
, where
\mathbf{I}_{\mathbf{\xi}j}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{I}}_{\mathbf{\xi}j}
are defined in Lemma
9
and in (
39
), respectively. Also, given
\mathbf{\widehat{H}}
defined in (
35
), we have
\mathbf{\widehat{H}}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}\mathbf{QV}^{-1}=\mathbf{H}
, where
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}=\textrm{p}\lim_{N,T\rightarrow\infty}\left(\mathbf{GG}/T\right)

by Assumption (
1
), and
\mathbf{Q}=\textrm{p}\lim_{N,T\rightarrow\infty}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{A}}/N\right)
by Lemma
6
. By Theorem
1
, we then have
\left(\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}\right)^{\prime}\overset{p}{\rightarrow}\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{\xi}}\left(\mathbf{B}_{1}\penalty\ \mathbf{B}_{2}\right)^{\prime}
. Therefore

\textrm{p}\lim_{N,T\rightarrow\infty}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}}{N}&\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}}{N}\\
\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}}{N}&\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{B}}_{2}}{N}\end{array}\right)=\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{\xi}}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{B}1}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{B}12}\\
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{B2}1}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{B}2}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{\xi}}^{\prime},

where, by Assumption
2
,
\left\|\left(\mathbf{B}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{B}_{j}/N\right)-\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{B}j}\right\|\rightarrow 0
and
\left\|\left(\mathbf{B}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{B}_{k}/N\right)-\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{B}jk}\right\|\rightarrow 0
, for
j,k=1,2
with
j\neq k
as
N\rightarrow\infty
. The result stated in the theorem follows by noting that

\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\mathbf{B}_{1}^{\prime}\\
\mathbf{B}_{2}^{\prime}\end{array}\right)\mathbf{e}_{t}\overset{d}{\rightarrow}\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{Be}t}\right).

by Assumption
6
(d), which concludes the proof.

B.5
Proof of Theorem
3

Given
r_{1}=r_{2}
, consider
j=1
: analogous arguments hold for
j=2
. We can then partition the vector
\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}
in (
4.2
) as

\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}=\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\left(1\right)}\\
\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\left(2\right)}\end{array}\right).

In this way, (
4.2
) itself may be written as

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\sqrt{T}\left\{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\prime}-\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11},\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,12}\right]-\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\left[\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right),\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{22}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,22}\right)\right]\right\}\\
=&\sqrt{T}\left\{\left(\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\left(1\right)\prime},\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\left(2\right)\prime}\right)^{\prime}-\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11},\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,12}\right]-\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\left[\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right),\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{22}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,22}\right)\right]\right\}\\
=&\sqrt{T}\left\{\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\left(1\right)\prime}-\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right),\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\left(2\right)\prime}-\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,12}-\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{22}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,22}\right)\right\}.\end{array}

Since it is known that
r_{1}=r_{2}
, the estimator
\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{1i}
for
\bm{\lambda}_{1i}
is equal to
\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\left(1\right)}
.
Formally, for
i=1,\ldots,N
, it follows that

\sqrt{T}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{b}}_{1i}^{\left(1\right)\prime}-\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]=\sqrt{T}\left[\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{1i}^{\prime}-\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right].

Given
\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}=\left(\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{11},\ldots,\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{1N}\right)^{\prime}
,
from (
45
) interest lies in

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{1t}&=&\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}\right)^{-1}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{x}_{t}\right)\\
&=&\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}\right)^{-1}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{x}_{t}\right)\\
&=&\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}\right)^{-1}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbb{I}_{1t}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbb{I}_{2t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)\right]\\
&=&\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}\right)^{-1}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\right)\left(\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\right)+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}\right)^{-1}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\right)\left(\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\right)\\
&&+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}\right)^{-1}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)\\
&=&\left(\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}}{N}\right)^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\right)\left(\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\right)+\left(\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}}{N}\right)^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\right)\left(\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\right)\\
&&+\left(\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}}{N}\right)^{-1}\left(\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}}{N}\right).\end{array}

(B.52)

Adding and subtracting terms, we have

\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}=\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right),

which implies that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}&=&\dfrac{\left[\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\\
&=&+\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}.\end{array}

Note that
\left.\left[\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]\right/N
is of the same
order as
\left.\left(\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\right)\right/N
. Therefore, by (
B.15
) it follows that

\dfrac{\left[\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right),

so that

\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}=\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).

(B.53)

Similarly,

\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}=\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).

(B.54)

Also,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}}{N}&=&\left[\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)}{\sqrt{N}}\right]^{\prime}\\
&&\times\left[\dfrac{\widehat{\mathbf{\Lambda}}_{1}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}-\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)}{\sqrt{N}}\right]\\
&=&\left[\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)}{\sqrt{N}}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{N}}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right]^{\prime}\\
&&\times\left[\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)}{\sqrt{N}}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{N}}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right]\\
&=&\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]}{N}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{N}}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).\end{array}

(B.55)

Therefore, taking into account (
B.52
) through (
B.55
) we have

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{1t}&=&\left\{\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]}{N}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{N}}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right\}^{-1}\\
&&\times\left\{\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\right\}\left(\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\right)\\
&&+\left\{\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]}{N}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{N}}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right\}^{-1}\\
&&\times\left\{\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\right\}\left(\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\right)\\
&&+\left\{\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]}{N}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{N}}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right)\right\}^{-1}\\
&&\times\left\{\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}}{N}\right\}\\
&&+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).\end{array}

It follows that,

\begin{array}[]{cl}&\sqrt{N}\left\{\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{1t}-\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\left\{\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]}{N}\right\}^{-1}\\
\times\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\right)}{N}\end{array}\right\}\right\}\\
=&\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}}{\sqrt{N}}+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{\sqrt{N}}{C_{NT}^{2}}\right).\end{array}

(B.56)

Consider

\begin{array}[]{cl}&\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\dfrac{\left[\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)\right]^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}}{\sqrt{N}}\\
=&\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{e}_{t}\right]\\
=&\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\left[\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}e_{it}+\left(\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}-\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}\right)^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}e_{it}\right].\end{array}

(B.57)

and let

\xi_{1,t}^{\ast}=p\lim_{N,T\rightarrow\infty}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}.

(B.58)

Further, from (
40
) recall that for
j=1,2
,

\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}=\widehat{\mathbf{H}}\widehat{\mathbf{I}}_{\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}j}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j,11}&\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j,12}\\
\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j,21}&\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j,22}\end{array}\right),

where
\widehat{\mathbf{H}}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{I}}_{\widehat{\mathbf{\xi}}j}
are defined in (
35
) and (
39
), respectively. Taking into account (
38
) and Lemma (
9
), it follows that

p\lim_{N,T\rightarrow\infty}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}=\mathbf{H\cdot I}_{\mathbf{\xi}j}=\mathbf{HH}^{-1}\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbb{I}\left(j=1\right)\mathbb{I}_{r_{1}}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbb{I}\left(j=2\right)\mathbb{I}_{r_{2}}\end{array}\right]\mathbf{H=}\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbb{I}\left(j=1\right)\mathbb{I}_{r_{1}}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbb{I}\left(j=2\right)\mathbb{I}_{r_{2}}\end{array}\right]\mathbf{H.}

Given (
38
), from (
28
) and (
36
),
recall the definitions of
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}
and
\mathbf{Q}
, respectively. We then have

\mathbf{H}=\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}\mathbf{QV}^{-1}=\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}\left(\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{-1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi V}^{1\left/2\right.}\right)\mathbf{V}^{-1}=\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi V}^{-1\left/2\right.},

which implies that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathbf{H}&=&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{g}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi V}^{-1\left/2\right.}\\
&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Psi}_{11}&\mathbf{\Psi}_{12}\\
\mathbf{\Psi}_{21}&\mathbf{\Psi}_{22}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{V}_{2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\end{array}\right)\\
&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{11}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{12}\\
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{21}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{22}\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{V}_{2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\end{array}\right)\\
&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{11}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{12}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\\
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{21}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{22}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\end{array}\right)\\
&=&\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{H}_{11}&\mathbf{H}_{12}\\
\mathbf{H}_{21}&\mathbf{H}_{22}\end{array}\right),\end{array}

where
\mathbf{H}_{jk}=p\lim_{N,T\rightarrow\infty}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{jk}
. Therefore,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}p\lim\limits_{N,T\rightarrow\infty}\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{j}&=&\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbb{I}\left(j=1\right)\mathbb{I}_{r_{1}}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbb{I}\left(j=2\right)\mathbb{I}_{r_{2}}\end{array}\right]\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{11}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{12}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\\
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{21}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{22}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\end{array}\right)\\
&=&\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbb{I}\left(j=1\right)\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{11}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbb{I}\left(j=1\right)\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{1}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{12}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\\
\mathbb{I}\left(j=2\right)\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{21}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{-1\left/2\right.}&\mathbb{I}\left(j=2\right)\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\mathbf{f}_{2}}^{1\left/2\right.}\mathbf{\Psi}_{22}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{-1\left/2\right.}\end{array}\right].\end{array}

Therefore, we have
\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,11}=\mathbf{H}_{11}+o_{p}\left(1\right)
and
\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{1,21}=o_{p}\left(1\right)
. Taking this into account in (
B.56
) and (
B.57
), and recalling (
B.58
), it follows that

\begin{array}[]{cl}&\sqrt{N}\left\{\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{1t}^{\left(1\right)}-\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\left[\dfrac{\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}\right)}{N}\right]^{-1}\\
\times\dfrac{\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\right)}{N}\end{array}\right\}\right\}\\
=&\xi_{1,t}^{\ast}\left(\mathbf{H}_{11}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}e_{it}+\mathbf{H}_{21}^{\prime}\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{N}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}e_{it}\right)+o_{p}\left(1\right).\end{array}

By Assumption (
6
)(d), it follows that

\begin{array}[]{cl}&\sqrt{N}\left\{\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{1t}-\left\{\begin{array}[]{c}\left[\dfrac{\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}\right)^{\prime}\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}\right)}{N}\right]^{-1}\\
\times\dfrac{\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{11}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{H}}_{21}\right)^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1,t\left|T\right.}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\right)}{N}\end{array}\right\}\right\}\\
\overset{d}{\rightarrow}&\mathcal{N}\left(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{1t}}\right),\end{array}

where

\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{1t}}=\left(\xi_{1,t}^{\ast}\right)^{2}\left(\mathbf{H}_{11}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Phi}_{1t}\mathbf{H}_{11}+\mathbf{H}_{11}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Phi}_{12t}\mathbf{H}_{21}+\mathbf{H}_{21}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Phi}_{12t}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{11}+\mathbf{H}_{22}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Phi}_{2t}\mathbf{H}_{22}\right),

with
\mathbf{\Phi}_{12t}
defined in Assumption
6
(d). This which completes the proof of the theorem.

B.6
Proof of Theorem
4

For
j=1,2
, consider the covariance matrix
\mathbf{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{\widehat{\xi},\mathbf{x}j}
defined in (
47
). By definition of eigenvectors and eigenvalues, it follows that
\mathbf{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{\widehat{\xi},\mathbf{x}j}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}=\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}
.
Recall the matrix
\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}^{\left(p\right)}
defined according to (
48
). We can then write

\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}-\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},jj}^{\left(p\right)}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}^{\left(p\right)}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}=\mathbf{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{\widehat{\xi},\mathbf{x}j}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}-\left(\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},jj}^{\left(p\right)}+\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}^{\left(p\right)}\right)\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)},

which implies that

\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)\prime}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},jj}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{\prime}\right)=\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{\widehat{\xi},\mathbf{x}j}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},jj}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}^{\prime}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{k}^{\prime}\right).

Without loss of generality, set
j=1
: the case
j=2
can be dealt with in a similar way. Since
\mathbf{x}_{t}=\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}
, and
x_{it}=\mathbb{I}_{1t}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{2t}+e_{it}
, we can write

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1i}^{\left(p\right)}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},21}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\right)\\
=&\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}x_{it}}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}-\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},21}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\right)\\
=&\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}\mathbf{f}_{2t}+\mathbf{e}_{t}\right)\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{1t}+\mathbb{I}_{2t}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{2t}+e_{it}\right)}{N\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\\
&-\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\dfrac{\mathbf{F}_{11}\mathbf{F}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}-\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\dfrac{\mathbf{F}_{22}\mathbf{F}_{\widehat{\xi},12}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\\
=&\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\right)\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\right)\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\\
&+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{it}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}e_{it}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\\
&+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\right)\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\right)\bm{\lambda}_{2i}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbf{e}_{t}e_{it}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\right)\\
&-\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\dfrac{\mathbf{F}_{11}\mathbf{F}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}-\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\dfrac{\mathbf{F}_{22}\mathbf{F}_{\widehat{\xi},12}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\\
=&\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\mathsf{E}\left(e_{lt}e_{it}\right)+\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left[e_{lt}e_{it}-\mathsf{E}\left(e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\\
&+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{it}}{T}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}e_{it}}{T}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\\
&+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}}{T}\right)\bm{\lambda}_{1i}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}}{T}\right)\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}},\end{array}

or equivalently

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1i}^{\left(p\right)}-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},21}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\right)\right]\\
=&\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)+\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\\
&+\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\\
&+\dfrac{1}{NT}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\\
&+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{1}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{it}}{T}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{2}}{N}\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}e_{it}}{T}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\\
&+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}^{\prime}}{T}\right)\bm{\lambda}_{1i}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}+\dfrac{\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)\prime}}{N}\left(\dfrac{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{e}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}^{\prime}}{T}\right)\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}},\end{array}

which is also equal to

\begin{array}[]{rrl}\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1i}^{\left(p\right)}-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},21}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\right)\right]&=&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left\{\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{1t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\right\}\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left\{\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{2t}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\right\}\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{1l}^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{it}\right)\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{2l}^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}e_{it}\right)\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{1t}\mathbf{f}_{1t}e_{lt}\right)\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\\
&&+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}\mathbb{I}_{2t}\mathbf{f}_{2t}e_{lt}\right)\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}.\end{array}

In general, for
j,k=1,2
define

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}=\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{it}e_{lt}\right),&&\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}=\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right],\\
&&\\
\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}=\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt},&&\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},jkli}=\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{it}.\end{array}

We can then write

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1i}^{\left(p\right)}-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},21}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\right)\right]\\
=&\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},11il}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},21il}\\
&+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},11il}+\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},21il}\\
&+\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},11il}\right)\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}+\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},21il}\right)\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}\\
&+\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},11li}\right)\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}+\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1l}^{\left(p\right)}\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},21li}\right)\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{1t\left|T\right.}}.\end{array}

For
j,k=1,2
note that

\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|\leq\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{F}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}\mathbf{F}_{jj}^{\prime}}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}}{N}\right\|\leq\dfrac{T}{\sum\nolimits_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\xi}_{jt\left|T\right.}}\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{F}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}\mathbf{F}_{jj}^{\prime}}{T}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{j}}{N}\right\|^{1\left/2\right.}\left\|\dfrac{\bm{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\widehat{\Lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},j}^{\left(p\right)}}{N}\right\|^{1\left/2\right.}=O_{p}\left(1\right)

by Assumptions
1
(b) and
2
(b). Since
\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{j}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|=O_{p}\left(1\right)
by Lemma
10
,
then
\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},kj}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|=O_{p}\left(1\right)
. It follows that

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1i}^{\left(p\right)}-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},21}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\right)\right]\right\|^{2}\leq 8\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left(\widehat{\sigma}_{\widehat{\xi},j1i\cdot}+\widehat{\chi}_{\widehat{\xi},j1i\cdot}+\widehat{\varphi}_{\widehat{\xi},j1i\cdot}+\widehat{\varphi}_{\widehat{\xi},j1\cdot i}\right),

(B.59)

where in general

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\widehat{\sigma}_{\widehat{\xi}jki\cdot}=\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right\|^{2},&&\widehat{\chi}_{\widehat{\xi},jki\cdot}=\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right\|^{2},\\
&&\\
\widehat{\varphi}_{\widehat{\xi},jki\cdot}=\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right\|^{2},&&\widehat{\varphi}_{\widehat{\xi}jk\cdot i}=\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},jkli}\right\|^{2}.\end{array}

Starting from
\widehat{\sigma}_{\widehat{\xi},jki\cdot}
, consider

\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right\|^{2}\leq\left(\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{kl}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}^{2}\right)

and

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\widehat{\sigma}_{\widehat{\xi},jki\cdot}\leq\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sigma_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}^{2}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\right)

(B.60)

by Assumption
2
(b) and Lemma
11
. As for
\widehat{\chi}_{\widehat{\xi}jki\cdot}
,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\widehat{\chi}_{\widehat{\xi},jki\cdot}&=&\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right\|^{2}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{N}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kq}^{\left(p\right)}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkiq}\\
&\leq&\left[\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{N}\left(\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kq}^{\left(p\right)}\right)^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\left[\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{N}\left(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkiq}\right)^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\\
&\leq&\left(\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|^{2}\right)\left[\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{N}\left(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkiq}\right)^{2}\right]^{1\left/2\right.}\end{array}

where

\mathsf{E}\left[\left(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkiq}\right)^{2}\right]=\mathsf{E}\left(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{q=1}^{N}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkiq}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkul}\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkuq}\right)\leq N^{2}\cdot\max_{i,l}\mathsf{E}\left|\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right|^{4},

and since

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\mathsf{E}\left|\chi_{\widehat{\xi},jkil}\right|^{4}&=&\mathsf{E}\left|\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}-\mathrm{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\right|^{4}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{T^{2}}\mathsf{E}\left|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\left[\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}-\mathsf{E}\left(\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}e_{it}\right)\right]\right|^{4}\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{T^{2}}M\end{array}

by Assumption
3
(c), and taking into account Assumption
2
(b),

\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\widehat{\chi}_{\widehat{\xi},jki\cdot}\leq O_{p}\left(1\right)\cdot\sqrt{\dfrac{N^{2}}{T^{2}}}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{N}{T}\right),

which implies that

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{2}\widehat{\chi}_{\widehat{\xi},jki\cdot}=\dfrac{1}{N}O_{p}\left(\dfrac{N}{T}\right)=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\right).

(B.61)

Further,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\widehat{\varphi}_{\widehat{\xi},jki\cdot}&=&\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\varphi_{\widehat{\xi}jkil}\right\|^{2}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}\right)\right\|^{2}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\bm{\lambda}_{ji}^{\prime}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}\right)\right\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{T}\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{ji}\right\|^{2}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|^{2}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{lt}\right\|^{2}\right)\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\right)\end{array}

(B.62)

by Assumptions
2
(a),
2
(b) and
4
. Finally,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\widehat{\varphi}_{\widehat{\xi},jk\cdot i}&=&\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\varphi_{\widehat{\xi},jkli}\right\|^{2}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{it}\right)\right\|^{2}\\
&=&\dfrac{1}{N^{2}}\left\|\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{it}\right)\right\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{T}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\bm{\lambda}_{jl}^{\prime}\right\|\left\|\dfrac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum\limits_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbb{I}_{jt}\widehat{\xi}_{kt\left|T\right.}e_{it}\right\|\right)^{2}\\
&\leq&\dfrac{1}{T}\left[\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\widehat{\bm{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},kl}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{l=1}^{N}\left\|\bm{\lambda}_{jl}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1\left/2\right.}O_{p}\left(1\right)\right]\\
&=&O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\right)\end{array}

(B.63)

by Assumptions
2
(b) and
4
. From equations (
B.59
) through (
B.63
) it follows that

\dfrac{1}{N}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\left[\bm{\widehat{\lambda}}_{\widehat{\xi},1i}^{\left(p\right)}-\left(\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},11}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{1i}+\mathbf{\widehat{H}}_{\widehat{\xi},21}^{\left(p\right)\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{2i}\right)\right]\right\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{N}\right)+O_{p}\left(\dfrac{1}{T}\right),

and since
\left\|\mathbf{\widehat{V}}_{\widehat{\xi},1}^{\left(p\right)}\right\|=O_{p}\left(1\right)
by Lemma
10
the result stated in the theorem follows.

Appendix C
Proof of result (
52
)

Consider

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)-\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)=

\displaystyle\,\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)-\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)

\displaystyle-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]+\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,\left\{\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle-\left\{\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]\right\}.

Since
\widehat{\mathbf{q}}
is the maximum likelihood estimator, it follows that

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\geq\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right),\end{array}

or, equivalently,

\begin{array}[]{rl}&\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)-\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\geq 0,\end{array}

which implies that

\displaystyle\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]\geq

\displaystyle\,\left\{\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle-\left\{\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\,o_{p}\left(1\right)-\left\{\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]\right\},

so that

\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]\geq o_{p}\left(1\right).

If
\widehat{\mathbf{q}}
was an estimator for
\mathbf{q}^{\left(3\right)}
, then

\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(3\right)}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]=o_{p}\left(1\right).

This implies that, for some
C>0
, and taking into account (
51
),

\displaystyle\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\widehat{\mathbf{q}}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=-\left\{\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(1\right)}\right)\right]-\mathsf{E}\left[\dfrac{1}{NT}\log f\left(\mathbf{X};\mathbf{q}^{\left(3\right)}\right)\right]\right\}+o_{p}\left(1\right)

\displaystyle=-C+o_{p}\left(1\right),

which leads to (
52
).

Appendix D
Additional simulation results

D.1
Change in the number of factors

We simulate the latent state
\bm{\xi}_{t}
according to (
5
), with
\mathbf{P}
having entries
p_{11}=0.9
and
p_{22}=0.7
, so that
p_{12}=0.1
and
p_{21}=0.3
. This configuration corresponds to the unconditional probabilities to be equal to
\mathsf{P}(s_{t}=1)=\mathsf{E}[\xi_{1t}]=\frac{1-p_{22}}{2-p_{11}-p_{22}}=0.75
and
\mathsf{P}(s_{t}=2)=\mathsf{E}[\xi_{2t}]=\frac{1-p_{11}}{2-p_{11}-p_{22}}=0.25
. Then, we generate the innovations
\mathbf{v}_{t}
of the VAR in (
5
) as follows: at each given
t
we generate
u_{t}\sim\mathcal{U}[0,1]
and

(i)

if
\xi_{1,t-1}=1
and
u_{t}\leq p_{11}
then
\mathbf{v}_{t}=[1\;0]^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t-1}
;

(ii)

if
\xi_{1,t-1}=1
and
u_{t}>p_{11}
then
\mathbf{v}_{t}=[0\;1]^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t-1}
;

(iii)

if
\xi_{1,t-1}=0
and
u_{t}\leq p_{21}
then
\mathbf{v}_{t}=[1\;0]^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t-1}
;

(iv)

if
\xi_{1,t-1}=0
and
u_{t}>p_{21}
then
\mathbf{v}_{t}=[0\;1]^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t-1}
.

We set the number of factors as
r_{1}=3
and
r_{2}=1
. The common component is generated according to model (
1
). Let
\chi_{it}=\bm{\lambda}_{1i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbb{I}(s_{t}=1)+\bm{\lambda}_{2i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbb{I}(s_{t}=2)
,
i=1,\ldots,N
,
t=1,\ldots,T
. The
r
entries of
\bm{\lambda}_{1i}
and
\bm{\lambda}_{2i}
are generated from a
\mathcal{N}(1,1)
distribution. The matrices
\bm{\Lambda}_{1}
and
\bm{\Lambda}_{2}
are then transformed in such a way that
\bm{\Lambda}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{1}
and
\bm{\Lambda}_{2}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}_{2}
are diagonal matrices. The factors are such that they satisfy
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{f}_{jt}\mathbf{f}_{jt}^{\prime}=\mathbf{I}_{r_{j}}
,
j=1,2
, where each component of
\mathbf{f}_{jt}
is such that
f_{j,kt}=\rho_{f}f_{j,k,t-1}+z_{j,kt}
,
k=1,\ldots,r_{j}
,
j=1,2
, with
\rho_{f}=\{0,0.7\}
and
z_{j,kt}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
.

The idiosyncratic components are generated according to (
2
), where
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{je}=\mathbf{\Sigma}_{je,a}+\mathbf{\Sigma}_{je,b}
,
j=1,2
, with
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{je,a}
diagonal and
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{je,b}
banded. Specifically, the entries of
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{1e,a}
are generated from a
\mathcal{U}[0.25,1.25]
and those of
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{2e,a}
are generated from a
\mathcal{U}[0.75,1.75]
, while
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{1e,b}
is a Toeplitz matrix with
\tau^{k}
on the
k
th diagonal for
k=1,2
and zero elsewhere, and, finally
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{2e,b}
is a Toeplitz matrix with
\tau^{k-1}
on the
k
th diagonal for
k=1,2,3
and zero elsewhere. We set
\tau=\{0,0.5\}
. Moreover, each component of
\bm{\nu}_{t}
is such that
\nu_{it}=\rho_{i}\nu_{i,t-1}+\omega_{it}
,
i=1,\ldots,N
,
t=1,\ldots,T
, with
\rho_{i}=\{0,\rho\}
and
\rho\sim\mathcal{U}[0,0.5]
. Finally, we set the average noise-to-signal ratio across all
N
simulated time series to be
N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{it}^{2}}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\chi_{it}^{2}}=0.5
.

,

D.2
Change in the autocorrelation of factors

We simulate the latent state
\bm{\xi}_{t}
according to (
5
), with
\mathbf{P}
having entries
p_{11}=0.9
and
p_{22}=0.7
, so that
p_{12}=0.1
and
p_{21}=0.3
. This configuration corresponds to the unconditional probabilities to be equal to
\mathsf{P}(s_{t}=1)=\mathsf{E}[\xi_{1t}]=\frac{1-p_{22}}{2-p_{11}-p_{22}}=0.75
and
\mathsf{P}(s_{t}=2)=\mathsf{E}[\xi_{2t}]=\frac{1-p_{11}}{2-p_{11}-p_{22}}=0.25
. Then, we generate the innovations
\mathbf{v}_{t}
of the VAR in (
5
) as follows: at each given
t
we generate
u_{t}\sim\mathcal{U}[0,1]
and

(i)

if
\xi_{1,t-1}=1
and
u_{t}\leq p_{11}
then
\mathbf{v}_{t}=[1\;0]^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t-1}
;

(ii)

if
\xi_{1,t-1}=1
and
u_{t}>p_{11}
then
\mathbf{v}_{t}=[0\;1]^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t-1}
;

(iii)

if
\xi_{1,t-1}=0
and
u_{t}\leq p_{21}
then
\mathbf{v}_{t}=[1\;0]^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t-1}
;

(iv)

if
\xi_{1,t-1}=0
and
u_{t}>p_{21}
then
\mathbf{v}_{t}=[0\;1]^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}^{\prime}\bm{\xi}_{t-1}
.

We set the number of factors in each state to
r_{j}=r=1
,
j=1,2
. The common component is generated according to model (
1
). Let
\chi_{it}=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{1t}\mathbb{I}(s_{t}=1)+\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{2t}\mathbb{I}(s_{t}=2)
,
i=1,\ldots,N
,
t=1,\ldots,T
. The
r
entries of
\bm{\lambda}_{i}
are generated from a
\mathcal{N}(1,1)
distribution. The matrix
\bm{\Lambda}
is then transformed in such a way that
\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}
is diagonal. The factors are such that
f_{1t}=0.9f_{1,t-1}+z_{1t}
and
f_{2t}=z_{2t}
with
z_{kt}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
,
k=1,2
, then
f_{1t}
is rescaled to have variance one.

The idiosyncratic components are generated having covariance matrix
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e}=\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,a}+\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,b}
, with
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,a}
diagonal and
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,b}
banded. Specifically, the entries of
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,a}
are generated from a
\mathcal{U}[0.25,1.25]
, while
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,b}
is a Toeplitz matrix with
\tau^{k}
on the
k
th diagonal for
k=1,2
and zero elsewhere. We set
\tau=\{0,0.5\}
. Moreover, each component of
\bm{\nu}_{t}
is such that
\nu_{it}=\rho_{i}\nu_{i,t-1}+\omega_{it}
,
i=1,\ldots,N
,
t=1,\ldots,T
, with
\rho_{i}=\{0,\rho\}
and
\rho\sim\mathcal{U}[0,0.5]
. Finally, we set the average noise-to-signal ratio across all
N
simulated time series to be
N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{it}^{2}}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\chi_{it}^{2}}=0.5
.

D.3
No change

We set the number of factors to
r=2
. The common component is generated according to
\chi_{it}=\bm{\lambda}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{t}
,
i=1,\ldots,N
,
t=1,\ldots,T
. The
r
entries of
\bm{\lambda}_{i}
are generated from a
\mathcal{N}(1,1)
distribution. The matrix
\bm{\Lambda}
is then transformed in such a way that
\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}
is diagonal. The factors are such that
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{f}_{t}\mathbf{f}_{t}^{\prime}=\mathbf{I}_{r}
, where each component of
\mathbf{f}_{t}
is such that
f_{kt}=\rho_{f}f_{k,t-1}+z_{kt}
,
k=1,\ldots,r
, with
\rho_{f}=\{0,0.7\}
and
z_{kt}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
.

The idiosyncratic components are generated having covariance matrix
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e}=\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,a}+\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,b}
, with
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,a}
diagonal and
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,b}
banded. Specifically, the entries of
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,a}
are generated from a
\mathcal{U}[0.25,1.25]
, while
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{e,b}
is a Toeplitz matrix with
\tau^{k}
on the
k
th diagonal for
k=1,2
and zero elsewhere. We set
\tau=\{0,0.5\}
. Moreover, each component of
\bm{\nu}_{t}
is such that
\nu_{it}=\rho_{i}\nu_{i,t-1}+\omega_{it}
,
i=1,\ldots,N
,
t=1,\ldots,T
, with
\rho_{i}=\{0,\rho\}
and
\rho\sim\mathcal{U}[0,0.5]
. Finally, we set the average noise-to-signal ratio across all
N
simulated time series to be
N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\frac{\sum_{t=1}^{T}e_{it}^{2}}{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\chi_{it}^{2}}=0.5
.

In this case, we report the following multiple
R^{2}
for the estimated loadings

R^{2}_{B}=\frac{\text{tr}\left\{\left(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\right)\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{B}}_{1}\right)^{-1}\left(\widehat{\mathbf{B}}_{1}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\right)\right\}}{\text{tr}\left(\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}\bm{\Lambda}\right)}.

No bias correction is necessary in this case, since no change is present in the true data generating process.
